([jejjohnson/pyrox](https://github.com/jejjohnson/pyrox/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/model-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **model-reviewer**: Read-only agent that evaluates model methodology — experiment tracking completeness, data leakage, evaluation rigor, failure mode documentation. ([niels-emmer/myace](https://github.com/niels-emmer/myace/blob/HEAD/collections/base/data-scientist/agents/model-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **model-reviewer**: Reviews a Bridge Town model change (a branch or pull request) before it is merged. ([Bridge-Town/claude-plugins](https://github.com/Bridge-Town/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/bridge-town/agents/model-reviewer.md))

Used as the final quality gate in multi-model pipelines. ([dynamic-dome/inception-sandbox](https://github.com/dynamic-dome/inception-sandbox/blob/HEAD/agents/model-reviewer.md))
- **model-reviewer**: Conducts adversarial Ivy specification reviews, hunting for logical gaps, missing invariants, and exploitable counterexample paths. ([ElNiak/panther-ivy-plugin](https://github.com/ElNiak/panther-ivy-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/panther-ivy-plugin/.backup/2026-04-28/agents/model-reviewer.md))
- **model-reviewer**: Validates the assembled value-stream model for completeness and referential integrity before render, then renders approve/reject — the LLM half of what validateModel checks mechanically. ([fairley46/Throughline](https://github.com/fairley46/Throughline/blob/HEAD/agents/model-reviewer.md))
- **model-reviewer**: Reviews a change to a project's BPMN graph: reads each changed process picture and its source against the spec and the modelling rules, and returns findings, editing nothing. Use after every change to models/&lt;project&gt;.bpmn, on the draft before it is written into the graph and before the owner reads the picture. Not for drawing the model (the bpmn-model skill), not for the C4 graph (qsm-c4's model-reviewer) and not for reviewing code (strict-reviewer). ([qsmark-labs/qsm-marketplace](https://github.com/qsmark-labs/qsm-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/qsm-bpmn/agents/model-reviewer.md))
- **model-reviewer**: Reviews a change to a project's C4 graph: reads each changed view and its LikeC4 source against the spec and the modelling rules, and returns findings, editing nothing. Use after every change to models/&lt;project&gt;.c4, on the draft before it is written into the graph and before the owner reads the views. Not for drawing the model (the c4-model skill), not for the BPMN graph (qsm-bpmn's model-reviewer) and not for reviewing code (strict-reviewer). - **model-reviewer**: Model reviewer: checks the draft portion of one document against the document's text before the portion is submitted with arch_submit — what the document states that the draft lacks, and what the draft claims that the document does not state. Read-only. Dispatch with: the path of the input directory built by the review-input command of the conport CLI. The session that submits the portion starts it, settles every finding and submits the portion with its review. - **model-right-sizer**: Model-selection economist — decides which Claude model each task in a flow should run on, independent of load, by weighing EFFECTIVENESS (capability the outcome needs) against EFFICIENCY (cost, latency, volume) and recommending the SMALLEST model that clears the bar. Grounds every call in live model IDs + per-token pricing retrieved at spawn — delegated to the cheapest capable tier rather than hand-maintained in this file, so a new model release never requires a manual edit here — instead of a static table that drifts as models ship.
